## Ben _2026-08-09 17:54:05 UTC_

# Code Review Plugin

Automated code review for pull requests using multiple specialized agents with confidence-based scoring to filter false positives.

## Overview

The Code Review Plugin automates pull request review by launching multiple agents in parallel to independently audit changes from different perspectives. It uses confidence scoring to filter out false positives, ensuring only high-quality, actionable feedback is posted.

## Commands

### `/code-review`

Performs automated code review on a pull request using multiple specialized agents.

**What it does:**
1. Checks if review is needed (skips closed, draft, trivial, or already-reviewed PRs)
2. Gathers relevant CLAUDE.md guideline files from the repository
3. Summarizes the pull request changes
4. Launches 4 parallel agents to independently review:
   - **Agents #1 & #2**: Audit for CLAUDE.md compliance
   - **Agent #3**: Scan for obvious bugs in changes
   - **Agent #4**: Analyze git blame/history for context-based issues
5. Scores each issue 0-100 for confidence level
6. Filters out issues below 80 confidence threshold
7. Outputs review (to terminal by default, or as PR comment with `--comment` flag)

**Usage:**

```bash
/code-review [--comment]
```

**Options:**
- `--comment`: Post the review as a comment on the pull request (default: outputs to terminal only)

**Example workflow:**

```bash
# On a PR branch, run locally (outputs to terminal):
/code-review

# Post review as PR comment:
/code-review --comment

# Claude will:
# - Launch 4 review agents in parallel
# - Score each issue for confidence
# - Output issues ≥80 confidence (to terminal or PR depending on flag)
# - Skip if no high-confidence issues found
```

**Features:**
- Multiple independent agents for comprehensive review
- Confidence-based scoring reduces false positives (threshold: 80)
- CLAUDE.md compliance checking with explicit guideline verification
- Bug detection focused on changes (not pre-existing issues)
- Historical context analysis via git blame
- Automatic skipping of closed, draft, or already-reviewed PRs
- Links directly to code with full SHA and line ranges

**Review comment format:**

```markdown
## Code review

Found 3 issues:

1. Missing error handling for OAuth callback (CLAUDE.md says "Always handle OAuth errors")

https://github.com/owner/repo/blob/abc123.../src/auth.ts#L67-L72

2. Memory leak: OAuth state not cleaned up (bug due to missing cleanup in finally block)

https://github.com/owner/repo/blob/abc123.../src/auth.ts#L88-L95

3. Inconsistent naming pattern (src/conventions/CLAUDE.md says "Use camelCase for functions")

https://github.com/owner/repo/blob/abc123.../src/utils.ts#L23-L28
```

**Confidence scoring:**
- **0**: Not confident, false positive
- **25**: Somewhat confident, might be real
- **50**: Moderately confident, real but minor
- **75**: Highly confident, real and important
- **100**: Absolutely certain, definitely real

**False positives filtered:**
- Pre-existing issues not introduced in PR
- Code that looks like a bug but isn't
- Pedantic nitpicks
- Issues linters will catch
- General quality issues (unless in CLAUDE.md)
- Issues with lint ignore comments

## Installation

This plugin is included in the Claude Code repository. The command is automatically available when using Claude Code.

## Best Practices

### Using `/code-review`
- Maintain clear CLAUDE.md files for better compliance checking
- Trust the 80+ confidence threshold - false positives are filtered
- Run on all non-trivial pull requests
- Review agent findings as a starting point for human review
- Update CLAUDE.md based on recurring review patterns

### When to use
- All pull requests with meaningful changes
- PRs touching critical code paths
- PRs from multiple contributors
- PRs where guideline compliance matters

### When not to use
- Closed or draft PRs (automatically skipped anyway)
- Trivial automated PRs (automatically skipped)
- Urgent hotfixes requiring immediate merge
- PRs already reviewed (automatically skipped)

## Workflow Integration

### Standard PR review workflow:

```bash
# Create PR with changes
# Run local review (outputs to terminal)
/code-review

# Review the automated feedback
# Make any necessary fixes

# Optionally post as PR comment
/code-review --comment

# Merge when ready
```

### As part of CI/CD:

```bash
# Trigger on PR creation or update
# Use --comment flag to post review comments
/code-review --comment
# Skip if review already exists
```

## Requirements

- Git repository with GitHub integration
- GitHub CLI (`gh`) installed and authenticated
- CLAUDE.md files (optional but recommended for guideline checking)

## Troubleshooting

### Review takes too long

**Issue**: Agents are slow on large PRs

**Solution**:
- Normal for large changes - agents run in parallel
- 4 independent agents ensure thoroughness
- Consider splitting large PRs into smaller ones

### Too many false positives

**Issue**: Review flags issues that aren't real

**Solution**:
- Default threshold is 80 (already filters most false positives)
- Make CLAUDE.md more specific about what matters
- Consider if the flagged issue is actually valid

### No review comment posted

**Issue**: `/code-review` runs but no comment appears

**Solution**:
Check if:
- PR is closed (reviews skipped)
- PR is draft (reviews skipped)
- PR is trivial/automated (reviews skipped)
- PR already has review (reviews skipped)
- No issues scored ≥80 (no comment needed)

### Link formatting broken

**Issue**: Code links don't render correctly in GitHub

**Solution**:
Links must follow this exact format:

```
https://github.com/owner/repo/blob/[full-sha]/path/file.ex
```

## Claude _2026-08-09 17:57:30 UTC_

Eklentiyi ekledim ve nasıl yapıldığını da repoya yazdım (`docs/eklenti-ekleme.md`). Özet cevap:

## Kendin eklenti nasıl eklersin

İş her zaman **iki adım**: önce *market* (katalog) eklenir, sonra o katalogdan eklenti kurulur.

**1. `/plugin` menüsü** — terminaldeki Claude Code'da en kolayı. Sekmeler: Discover / Installed / Marketplaces / Errors. Bir eklentiye Enter deyince kapsam seçersin: **User** (her projede sen), **Project** (repodaki herkes), **Local** (sadece sen, bu repo).

**2. Tek satırlık komutlar**
```
/plugin marketplace add anthropics/claude-code
/plugin install code-review@claude-code-plugins
/reload-plugins
```

**3. `.claude/settings.json`** — bizim burada kullandığımız yol. Sen web/bulut oturumundasın, orada `/plugin` paneli açılmıyor; ayrıca konteyner geçici, o yüzden tek kalıcı yöntem bu:
```json
"extraKnownMarketplaces": { "claude-code-plugins": { "source": { "source": "github", "repo": "anthropics/claude-code" } } },
"enabledPlugins": { "code-review@claude-code-plugins": true }
```
`enabledPlugins` içindeki `@` sonrası, `extraKnownMarketplaces` içinde senin verdiğin market adıyla aynı olmalı — tek dikkat edilecek nokta bu.

## Bu oturumda yaptıklarım

- `.claude/settings.json` → `code-review@claude-code-plugins` açıldı (`frontend-design`'ın yanına).
- `docs/eklenti-ekleme.md` → üç yöntem, mevcut marketler (`claude-plugins-official`, `claude-community`, `claude-code-plugins`), kendi eklentini yazma yapısı, güvenlik ve sık takılınan hatalar tablosu.
- `CLAUDE.md` → eklenti listesi + karar kaydı.

Hepsi `claude/code-review-plugin-jvgabq` dalına push edildi. PR açmadım — istersen açarım.

İki not: eklenti komutu **ad alanlı** çağrılıyor, yani `/code-review` değil `/code-review:code-review`. Ve bu ayar yeni oturumda etkin olacak — Claude Code eklentiyi kurmanı isterse sana `claude plugin install` komutunu gösterir.

=== DIFF: .claude/hooks/eklentileri-kur.sh ===
@@ -0,0 +1,111 @@
+#!/usr/bin/env bash
+# Konteyner gecici oldugu icin ~/.claude altina kurulan eklentiler her yeni
+# oturumda kaybolur. Bu hook onlari geri yukler.
+#
+# Kapsam:
+#   - gstack       (garrytan/gstack)      -> ~/.claude/skills/gstack
+#   - claude-mem   (thedotmack/claude-mem)-> ~/.claude/plugins + worker
+#   - Playwright Chromium takmasi         -> /opt/pw-browsers (indirme bloklu)
+#
+# Zaten kuruluysa saniyeler icinde cikar. Hicbir kosulda oturumu dusurmez.
+
+set -uo pipefail
+
+GSTACK_DIR="$HOME/.claude/skills/gstack"
+PW_DIR="${PLAYWRIGHT_BROWSERS_PATH:-/opt/pw-browsers}"
+LOG="${TMPDIR:-/tmp}/eklentileri-kur.log"
+: >"$LOG"
+
+durum=()
+
+# ── 1. Playwright Chromium takmasi ────────────────────────────
+# Konteynerde chromium-1194 kurulu ama gstack'in playwright'i 1208 ariyor ve
+# cdn.playwright.dev ag politikasi tarafindan blokli. Mevcut binary'yi
+# 1208'in bekledigi Chrome-for-Testing yerlesimiyle isaretliyoruz.
+pw_takla() {
+  local kaynak_c kaynak_h
+  kaynak_c=$(ls -d "$PW_DIR"/chromium-[0-9]* 2>/dev/null | grep -v -- '-1208$' | head -1)
+  kaynak_h=$(ls -d "$PW_DIR"/chromium_headless_shell-[0-9]* 2>/dev/null | grep -v -- '-1208$' | head -1)
+  [ -z "$kaynak_c" ] && return 1
+
+  if [ ! -e "$PW_DIR/chromium-1208/chrome-linux64/chrome" ]; then
+    mkdir -p "$PW_DIR/chromium-1208" || return 1
+    ln -sfn "$kaynak_c/chrome-linux" "$PW_DIR/chromium-1208/chrome-linux64"
+    touch "$PW_DIR/chromium-1208/INSTALLATION_COMPLETE" \
+          "$PW_DIR/chromium-1208/DEPENDENCIES_VALIDATED"
+  fi
+
+  if [ -n "$kaynak_h" ] && [ ! -e "$PW_DIR/chromium_headless_shell-1208/chrome-headless-shell-linux64/chrome-headless-shell" ]; then
+    ln -sfn headless_shell "$kaynak_h/chrome-linux/chrome-headless-shell"
+    mkdir -p "$PW_DIR/chromium_headless_shell-1208"
+    ln -sfn "$kaynak_h/chrome-linux" "$PW_DIR/chromium_headless_shell-1208/chrome-headless-shell-linux64"
+    touch "$PW_DIR/chromium_headless_shell-1208/INSTALLATION_COMPLETE" \
+          "$PW_DIR/chromium_headless_shell-1208/DEPENDENCIES_VALIDATED"
+  fi
+  return 0
+}
+pw_takla >>"$LOG" 2>&1
+
+# ── 2. gstack ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
+if [ -f "$GSTACK_DIR/SKILL.md" ] && [ -x "$GSTACK_DIR/browse/dist/browse" ]; then
+  durum+=("gstack: kurulu")
+elif command -v bun >/dev/null 2>&1; then
+  {
+    if [ ! -d "$GSTACK_DIR/.git" ]; then
+      rm -rf "$GSTACK_DIR"
+      git clone --single-branch --depth 1 \
+        https://github.com/garrytan/gstack.git "$GSTACK_DIR"
+    fi
+    cd "$GSTACK_DIR" && GSTACK_SKIP_FONTS=1 \
+      ./setup --host claude --prefix --no-team --no-plan-tune-hooks --quiet
+  } >>"$LOG" 2>&1
+  if [ -f "$GSTACK_DIR/SKILL.md" ]; then
+    durum+=("gstack: kuruldu")
+  else
+    durum+=("gstack: KURULAMADI ($LOG)")
+  fi
+else
+  durum+=("gstack: atlandi (bun yok)")
+fi
+
+# ── 3. claude-mem ─────────────────────────────────────────────
+if command -v npx >/dev/null 2>&1; then
+  if [ ! -d "$HOME/.claude/plugins/marketplaces/thedotmack" ]; then
+    npx --yes claude-mem@latest install >>"$LOG" 2>&1
+  fi
+  if [ -d "$HOME/.claude/plugins/marketplaces/thedotmack" ]; then
+    # Worker ayakta degilse baslat; zaten ayaktaysa no-op.
+    npx --yes claude-mem start >>"$LOG" 2>&1
+    durum+=("claude-mem: hazir")
+  else
+    durum+=("claude-mem: KURULAMADI ($LOG)")
+  fi
+else
+  durum+=("claude-mem: atlandi (npx yok)")
+fi
+
+# ── 4. Market eklentileri ─────────────────────────────────────
+# settings.json'daki extraKnownMarketplaces/enabledPlugins tek basina yetmiyor:
+# dis kaynakli eklenti gercekten kurulmadan yuklenmiyor. Eksikse kuruyoruz.
+if command -v claude >/dev/null 2>&1; then
+  if ! claude plugin marketplace list 2>/dev/null | grep -q "claude-code-plugins"; then
+    claude plugin marketplace add anthropics/claude-code >>"$LOG" 2>&1
+  fi
+  eksik=()
+  for p in security-guidance code-review frontend-design; do
+    if ! claude plugin list 2>/dev/null | grep -q "$p@claude-code-plugins"; then
+      claude plugin install "$p@claude-code-plugins" --scope project >>"$LOG" 2>&1
+      eksik+=("$p")
+    fi
+  done
+  if [ ${#eksik[@]} -eq 0 ]; then
+    durum+=("market eklentileri: kurulu")
+  else
+    durum+=("market eklentileri: kuruldu (${eksik[*]})")
+  fi
+else
+  durum+=("market eklentileri: atlandi (claude CLI yok)")
+fi
+
+printf 'Eklenti durumu — %s\n' "$(IFS='; '; echo "${durum[*]}")"
+exit 0

## Claude _2026-08-09 18:25:49 UTC_

Üçü de kurulu, çalışıyor ve test edildi. Push edildi.

## Test sonuçları

**1. security-guidance** (`@claude-code-plugins` v2.0.0) — ✅
- SessionStart hook'u çalıştı, Agent SDK'yı 562 ms'de hazırladı.
- Gerçek zararlı kodla denedim (`subprocess.call(..., shell=True)` + gömülü API anahtarı): komut enjeksiyonunu yakaladı, güvenli alternatifi döndürdü. Slash komutu yok — arka planda her düzenlemede çalışıyor.

**2. claude-mem** (`@thedotmack` v13.14.0) — ✅
- Worker 37700 portunda ayakta, HTTP 200. `~/.claude-mem` altında SQLite + chroma oluştu.
- ~20 beceri + 14 MCP arama aracı yüklendi.
- Fonksiyonel test: bu oturumun gözlemlerini şimdiden kaydetmiş; arama yaptığımda 6 sonuç döndürdü (ör. "Marketplace plugins auto-installation in startup hook").

**3. gstack** (v1.61.0.0) — ✅
- 54 beceri kuruldu, hepsi `gstack-` önekli (`/gstack-qa`, `/gstack-ship`, `/gstack-review`…). Öneki bilerek seçtim: diğer eklentilerle çakışmasın diye.
- `browse` binary'si uçtan uca test edildi: sayfa yükledi (HTTP 200), metin çıkardı, ekran görüntüsü aldı — görüntü doğru render ediyor.

## Yol boyunca çıkan 3 gerçek sorun ve çözümleri

**a) `settings.json` tek başına yetmiyormuş.** Marketi ve eklentiyi ayara yazmak onları *açıyor* ama *kurmuyor*. `claude plugin marketplace list` boştu — yani önceki oturumda kurduğum `frontend-design` aslında hiç aktif olmamış. `claude plugin install ... --scope project` ile üçünü de gerçekten diske kurdum.

**b) Playwright Chromium indirmesi bloklu.** gstack'in setup'ı ilk denemede burada patladı ve asıl skill kurulum adımları hiç çalışmadı. Konteynerde chromium-1194 var, gstack'in playwright'i 1208 istiyor, `cdn.playwright.dev` ise ağ politikasınca reddediliyor. Mevcut binary'yi 1208'in beklediği Chrome-for-Testing yerleşimiyle `/opt/pw-browsers` altına symlink'leyip setup'ı tekrar çalıştırdım — exit 0.

**c) Konteyner geçici.** `~/.claude` altındaki her şey oturum bitince gider. `.claude/hooks/eklentileri-kur.sh` yazdım: gstack'i, claude-mem'i, market eklentilerini ve Chromium taklasını geri yüklüyor. Her şey yerindeyse ~5 saniyede çıkıyor. Bu oturumda gerçekten tetiklendiğini gördüm: *"Eklenti durumu — gstack: kurulu; claude-mem: hazır; market eklentileri: kurulu"*.

## Bilmen gereken iki sınır

Bunlar kurulum hatası değil, ortam kısıtı:

- **Dış web gezintisi kapalı.** `gstack-browse` yerel sunucuya ve izinli hostlara gidiyor, ama `example.com` proxy'den 403 alıyor — `curl` de aynı sonucu veriyor. Yani `/gstack-qa` gibi canlı site test eden beceriler burada kendi çalıştırdığın uygulamada işe yarar, rastgele internet sitelerinde yaramaz.
- **Headed (görünür) tarayıcı yok.** Ekran sunucusu olmadığı için sadece headless çalışıyor. `/gstack-connect-chrome` bu ortamda kullanılamaz.

Ayrıca claude-mem `claude plugin list`'te iki kez görünüyor (user + project scope) — `npx` kurulumu user'a, ben project'e yazdığım için. Aynı sürüm, zararsız; project scope repoda kalsın diye bıraktım.

## Claude _2026-08-09 20:48:22 UTC_

I've read the entire file. Let me trace the new code:

**Phase 1 — Entry/sinks introduced by diff:**
- Entry: none — this hook runs at session start with no external inputs.
- Sinks: `node install.js` (arbitrary code exec) and `ls -d`/glob path building.

**Phase 2 — Data-flow analysis:**

1. `kok=$(ls -d "$HOME"/.claude/plugins/cache/thedotmack/claude-mem/*/ 2>/dev/null | head -1)` — glob is scoped to the user's own home directory. `$HOME` is trusted-env. No attacker-controlled input reaches this.
2. `cli="$kok/node_modules/tree-sitter-cli"` — path derived from same trusted glob.
3. `[ -f "$cli/install.js" ] || return 1` — existence gate.
4. `(cd "$cli" && node install.js) >>"$LOG" 2>&1` — runs an install script from within the claude-mem npm cache. That package was installed one step earlier via `npx --yes claude-mem@latest install`; if it's malicious, RCE was already achieved by the prior step. This diff introduces no additional trust boundary.

**Phase 2b — Parser/validator differentials:** none — no parsing added.

**Phase 2c — High-miss patterns:**
- No sensitive data logged (stdout/stderr of a node install script — no user secrets flow in).
- No IaC/CI trust/allowlist/gate changes.
- No permission/identity grants.
- No shell-metachar interpolation of attacker-controlled input (glob uses `$HOME`).
- The `$LOG` symlink-in-/tmp concern (`: >"$LOG"`) is pre-existing (line 17), not introduced by this diff.
- Binary fetched by `install.js` from GitHub releases isn't pinned to a version here, but the version is whatever the trusted claude-mem package's `node_modules/tree-sitter-cli` shipped with — the diff neither loosens nor tightens that.

**Verdict:** The new block just re-runs the postinstall step of an already-installed npm package inside the user's own cache. No attacker-influenceable source reaches any new sink, and no sibling gate is bypassed. Nothing meeting the reporting bar.

## Claude _2026-08-09 21:09:01 UTC_

I've read the changed file in full. The diff adds a `gh_kur` function that downloads the `gh` CLI binary from GitHub Releases and installs it as an executable that will subsequently be invoked with `GH_TOKEN` in the environment (as the added comment explicitly states).

Analysis of the new lines (110-132):

**Sink**: `install -m755 "$t/gh_${v}_linux_amd64/bin/gh" "$HOME/.local/bin/gh"` — writing an executable that will be run by 17 downstream skills with `GH_TOKEN` in-env.

**Source**: `curl` from `github.com/cli/cli/releases/download/...` followed by `tar xzf`. No SHA256/signature verification against a pinned digest.

**Other candidates I ruled out**:
- Path traversal via `tar`: extraction target `$t` is a fresh `mktemp -d`, then only `$t/gh_${v}_linux_amd64/bin/gh` is copied out via `install` (which follows symlinks but reads/writes a single file). The tarball could plant a symlink at that path pointing to another user-readable file, but the write destination is fixed at `$HOME/.local/bin/gh` — the concrete impact vs. plain-download is minimal.
- `command -v gh` shadowing: pre-existing behavior; not introduced by this diff.
- Log leak of `GH_TOKEN`: `curl -fsSL` is silent on success, and `GH_TOKEN` isn't referenced in the added code — not applicable here.
- Env/quoting: `$v` is a hardcoded literal `2.63.2`; no attacker-influenced interpolation into the URL/paths.

The one concrete, diff-introduced weakness worth reporting is the missing integrity check on the downloaded binary.

## Claude _2026-08-09 21:10:18 UTC_

I've read the full file. The new code (lines 110-132) is a small function that:
1. Skips if `gh` is already in PATH
2. Downloads gh CLI tarball over HTTPS from a hardcoded GitHub URL
3. Extracts it and installs to `$HOME/.local/bin/gh`

Let me consider the Phase 2c patterns applied to the + lines:

- **Command injection**: `v=2.63.2` is a literal, `$t` is `mktemp -d` output — no attacker-controlled interpolation into curl/tar/install.
- **Tar path escape**: `tar xzf "$t/gh.tgz" -C "$t"` — extraction is scoped to a fresh mktemp dir; only the specific `bin/gh` path is later installed, and any escape reduces to the excluded supply-chain concern (compromised tarball).
- **Secret leakage to LOG**: `curl` uses no auth headers (no `-u`, no `-H`), so `GH_TOKEN` cannot appear in curl error output that goes to `$LOG`. The `durum+=("gh: kurulamadi ($LOG)")` message is a path, not content.
- **PATH shadowing / binary planting**: install target is under `$HOME/.local/bin/` — the user's own dir, not a shared/privileged location.
- **Fail-open / gate mismatch**: no security decision paths added; the function returns 0/1 for install status only.
- **Sensitive-to-observability on the status printf**: outputs a hardcoded status string with the log path — no user or model data.
- **Cleanup / TOCTOU**: `rm -rf "$t"` runs on success or failure; `$t` is always set by the `t=$(mktemp -d) || return 1` guard before the `rm`.
- **Redirect / SSRF**: URL is a hardcoded literal; `-L` following GitHub CDN redirects is expected.

No sink/source pair beyond the already-flagged supply-chain-integrity issue is present in the new lines.

+KIMLIK_DESENLERI = re.compile(r"\b(msg_bdrk|toolu|msg|req)_[A-Za-z0-9]{20,}\b")
+
+
+def kimlikleri_kisalt(metin: str) -> str:
+    """Arac/mesaj kimliklerini onekini koruyarak sadelestirir."""
+    return KIMLIK_DESENLERI.sub(lambda m: f"{m.group(1)}_<ID>", metin)
+
+

Aksi halde GitHub'in push\n# korumasi butun push'u reddediyor (ve hakli olarak).\nGIZLI_DESENLER = re.compile(\n    r\"\"\"(\n        sk-ant-[A-Za-z0-9_-]{16,}          # Anthropic\n      | sk_live_[A-Za-z0-9]{16,}           # Stripe canli\n      | sk_test_[A-Za-z0-9]{16,}           # Stripe test\n      | rk_live_[A-Za-z0-9]{16,}           # Stripe kisitli\n      | AKIA[0-9A-Z]{16}                   # AWS erisim anahtari\n      | ASIA[0-9A-Z]{16}                   # AWS gecici anahtar\n      | gh[pousr]_[A-Za-z0-9]{20,}         # GitHub token\n      | github_pat_[A-Za-z0-9_]{20,}       # GitHub ince taneli PAT\n      | glpat-[A-Za-z0-9_-]{16,}           # GitLab\n      | xox[abprs]-[A-Za-z0-9-]{10,}       # Slack\n      | AIza[0-9A-Za-z_-]{35}              # Google API\n      | -----BEGIN[ A-Z]*PRIVATE\\ KEY----- # Ozel anahtar blogu\n    )\"\"\",\n    re.VERBOSE,\n)\n\n\ndef gizlileri_maskele(metin: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Anahtar gorunumlu dizileri maskeler; kalanini oldugu gibi birakir.\"\"\"\n    return GIZLI_DESENLER.sub(\"<REDACTED>\", metin)\n\n\ndef metin_bloklari(icerik) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Bir mesajin icerigindeki duz metin bloklarini birlestirir.\n\n    Arac cagrilari (tool_use / tool_result) ve dusunme bloklari atlanir --\n    amac konusmanin kendisini saklamak, her ara adimi degil.\n    \"\"\"\n    if isinstance(icerik, str):\n        return icerik.strip()\n    if not isinstance(icerik, list):\n        return \"\"\n    parcalar = []\n    for blok in icerik:\n        if isinstance(blok, dict) and blok.get(\"type\") == \"text\":\n            parcalar.append(str(blok.get(\"text\", \"\")).strip())\n    return \"\\n\\n\".join(p for p in parcalar if p)\n\n\ndef turlari_oku(transcript: Path):\n    \"\"\"Transcript jsonl dosyasini (rol, zaman, metin) uclulerine cevirir.\"\"\"\n    turler = []\n    with transcript.open(encoding=\"utf-8\", errors=\"replace\") as f:\n        for satir in f:\n            satir = satir.strip()\n            if not satir:\n                continue\n            try:\n                kayit = json.loads(satir)\n            except json.JSONDecodeError:\n                continue\n            if kayit.get(\"isSidechain\") or kayit.get(\"isMeta\"):\n                continue\n            tur = kayit.get(\"type\")\n            if tur not in (\"user\", \"assistant\"):\n                continue\n            mesaj = kayit.get(\"message\") or {}\n            metin = metin_bloklari(mesaj.get(\"content\"))\n            if not metin:\n                continue\n            # Arac sonuclarini tasiyan sistem hatirlatmalarini disarida birak.\n            if metin.startswith(\"<system-reminder>\") or metin.startswith(\"<command-name>\"):\n                continue\n            turler.append((tur, kayit.get(\"timestamp\", \"\"), metin))\n    return turler\n\n\ndef markdown_uret(turler, oturum_id: str, tarih: str) -> str:\n    satirlar = [\n        f\"# Oturum {tarih}\",\n        \"\",\n        f\"- Oturum kimligi: `{oturum_id}`\",\n        f\"- Son guncelleme: {datetime.now(timezone.utc):%Y-%m-%d %H:%M} UTC\",\n        f\"- Tur sayisi: {len(turler)}\",\n        \"\",\n        \"---\",\n        \"\",\n    ]\n    for rol, zaman, metin in turler:\n        baslik = \"Ben\" if rol == \"user\" else \"Claude\"\n        damga = f\" _{zaman[:19].replace('T', ' ')} UTC_\" if zaman else \"\"\n        satirlar.append(f\"## {baslik}{damga}\")\n        satirlar.append(\"\")\n        satirlar.append(metin)\n        satirlar.append(\"\")\n    return \"\\n\".join(satirlar).rstrip() + \"\\n\"\n\n\ndef indeksi_guncelle(gunluk: Path, dosya_adi: str, tarih: str, ozet: str) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Konusma gunlugundeki isaretli blogu tarihe gore siralayarak gunceller.\"\"\"\n    if not gunluk.exists():\n        return\n    icerik = gunluk.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\")\n    if INDEX_BASLANGIC not in icerik or INDEX_BITIS not in icerik:\n        return\n\n    bas = icerik.index(INDEX_BASLANGIC) + len(INDEX_BASLANGIC)\n    son = icerik.index(INDEX_BITIS)\n    mevcut = [s for s in icerik[bas:son].strip().splitlines() if s.strip().startswith(\"-\")]\n\n    yeni_satir = f\"- {tarih} — [{dosya_adi}](oturumlar/{dosya_adi}) — {ozet}\"\n    mevcut = [s for s in mevcut if f\"(oturumlar/{dosya_adi})\" not in s]\n    mevcut.append(yeni_satir)\n    mevcut.sort(reverse=True)\n\n    yeni = icerik[:bas] + \"\\n\\n\" + \"\\n\".join(mevcut) + \"\\n\\n\" + icerik[son:]\n    gunluk.write_text(yeni, encoding=\"utf-8\")\n\n\ndef ozet_uret(turler) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Indekste gorunecek kisa etiket: ilk kullanici mesajinin bas kismi.\"\"\"\n    for rol, _, metin in turler:\n        if rol == \"user\":\n            tek_satir = re.sub(r\"\\s+\", \" \", metin).strip()\n            return tek_satir[:80] + (\"…\" if len(tek_satir) > 80 else \"\")\n    return \"(bos oturum)\"\n\n\ndef git(kok: Path, *argumanlar) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess:\n    return subprocess.run(\n        [\"git\", *argumanlar], cwd=kok, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=120\n    )\n\n\ndef gonder(kok: Path, oturum_id: str) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Sadece kayit dosyalarini commit'leyip uzak dala (file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)"}]},"uuid":"5896202a-003c-4ecc-be2a-5aa743ba26c4","timestamp":"2026-08-09T21:47:47.512Z","toolUseResult":{"filePath":"/home/user/cloud/.claude/hooks/oturum-kaydet.py","oldString":"    for rol, _, metin in turler:\n        if rol == \"user\":\n            tek_satir = re.sub(r\"\\s+\", \" \", metin).strip()","newString":"    for rol, _, metin in turler:\n        if rol == \"user\":\n            # Kirpmadan once maskele: 80 karakterde kesilen bir anahtar artik\n            # desene uymaz ve yarisi indekste kalirdi.\n            tek_satir = re.sub(r\"\\s+\", \" \", gizlileri_maskele(metin)).strip()","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Oturum kaydedici hook.\n\nClaude Code'un Stop / SessionEnd olaylarinda calisir. stdin'den gelen hook\nJSON'undaki transcript dosyasini okur ve iki sey uretir:\n\n  docs/oturumlar/<tarih>-<oturum>.md    okunabilir konusma dokumu\n  docs/oturumlar/ham/<tarih>-<oturum>.jsonl   ham transcript yedegi\n\nAyrica docs/konusma-gunlugu.md dosyasindaki oturum indeksini gunceller.\n\n--push verildiginde (SessionEnd) yazilan dosyalari commit'leyip uzak dala\ngonderir. Sadece docs/oturumlar ve docs/konusma-gunlugu.md yollarini\nstage'ler, boylece devam eden calismadaki dosyalara dokunmaz.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport os\nimport re\nimport subprocess\nimport sys\nfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nINDEX_BASLANGIC = \"<!-- oturumlar:baslangic -->\"\nINDEX_BITIS = \"<!-- oturumlar:bitis -->\"\n\n\ndef repo_koku() -> Path:\n    \"\"\"Depo kokunu bul: once CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR, sonra script konumu.\"\"\"\n    ortam = os.environ.get(\"CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR\")\n    if ortam and (Path(ortam) / \".git\").exists():\n        return Path(ortam)\n    return Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]\n\n\n# Konusma dokumu repoya push edildigi icin, konusmada gecen anahtar\n# gorunumlu diziler yazilmadan once maskelenir. Aksi halde GitHub'in push\n# korumasi butun push'u reddediyor (ve hakli olarak).\nGIZLI_DESENLER = re.compile(\n    r\"\"\"(\n        sk-ant-[A-Za-z0-9_-]{16,}          # Anthropic\n      | sk_live_[A-Za-z0-9]{16,}           # Stripe canli\n      | sk_test_[A-Za-z0-9]{16,}           # Stripe test\n      | rk_live_[A-Za-z0-9]{16,}           # Stripe kisitli\n      | AKIA[0-9A-Z]{16}                   # AWS erisim anahtari\n      | ASIA[0-9A-Z]{16}                   # AWS gecici anahtar\n      | gh[pousr]_[A-Za-z0-9]{20,}         # GitHub token\n      | github_pat_[A-Za-z0-9_]{20,}       # GitHub ince taneli PAT\n      | glpat-[A-Za-z0-9_-]{16,}           # GitLab\n      | xox[abprs]-[A-Za-z0-9-]{10,}       # Slack\n      | AIza[0-9A-Za-z_-]{35}              # Google API\n      | -----BEGIN[ A-Z]*PRIVATE\\ KEY----- # Ozel anahtar blogu\n    )\"\"\",\n    re.VERBOSE,\n)\n\n\ndef gizlileri_maskele(metin: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Anahtar gorunumlu dizileri maskeler; kalanini oldugu gibi birakir.\"\"\"\n    return GIZLI_DESENLER.sub(\"<REDACTED>\", metin)\n\n\ndef metin_bloklari(icerik) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Bir mesajin icerigindeki duz metin bloklarini birlestirir.\n\n    Arac cagrilari (tool_use / tool_result) ve dusunme bloklari atlanir --\n    amac konusmanin kendisini saklamak, her ara adimi degil.\n    \"\"\"\n    if isinstance(icerik, str):\n        return icerik.strip()\n    if not isinstance(icerik, list):\n        return \"\"\n    parcalar = []\n    for blok in icerik:\n        if isinstance(blok, dict) and blok.get(\"type\") == \"text\":\n            parcalar.append(str(blok.get(\"text\", \"\")).strip())\n    return \"\\n\\n\".join(p for p in parcalar if p)\n\n\ndef turlari_oku(transcript: Path):\n    \"\"\"Transcript jsonl dosyasini (rol, zaman, metin) uclulerine cevirir.\"\"\"\n    turler = []\n    with transcript.open(encoding=\"utf-8\", errors=\"replace\") as f:\n        for satir in f:\n            satir = satir.strip()\n            if not satir:\n                continue\n            try:\n                kayit = json.loads(satir)\n            except json.JSONDecodeError:\n                continue\n            if kayit.get(\"isSidechain\") or kayit.get(\"isMeta\"):\n                continue\n            tur = kayit.get(\"type\")\n            if tur not in (\"user\", \"assistant\"):\n                continue\n            mesaj = kayit.get(\"message\") or {}\n            metin = metin_bloklari(mesaj.get(\"content\"))\n            if not metin:\n                continue\n            # Arac sonuclarini tasiyan sistem hatirlatmalarini disarida birak.\n            if metin.startswith(\"<system-reminder>\") or metin.startswith(\"<command-name>\"):\n                continue\n            turler.append((tur, kayit.get(\"timestamp\", \"\"), metin))\n    return turler\n\n\ndef markdown_uret(turler, oturum_id: str, tarih: str) -> str:\n    satirlar = [\n        f\"# Oturum {tarih}\",\n        \"\",\n        f\"- Oturum kimligi: `{oturum_id}`\",\n        f\"- Son guncelleme: {datetime.now(timezone.utc):%Y-%m-%d %H:%M} UTC\",\n        f\"- Tur sayisi: {len(turler)}\",\n        \"\",\n        \"---\",\n        \"\",\n    ]\n    for rol, zaman, metin in turler:\n        baslik = \"Ben\" if rol == \"user\" else \"Claude\"\n        damga = f\" _{zaman[:19].replace('T', ' ')} UTC_\" if zaman else \"\"\n        satirlar.append(f\"## {baslik}{damga}\")\n        satirlar.append(\"\")\n        satirlar.append(metin)\n        satirlar.append(\"\")\n    return \"\\n\".join(satirlar).rstrip() + \"\\n\"\n\n\ndef indeksi_guncelle(gunluk: Path, dosya_adi: str, tarih: str, ozet: str) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Konusma gunlugundeki isaretli blogu tarihe gore siralayarak gunceller.\"\"\"\n    if not gunluk.exists():\n        return\n    icerik = gunluk.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\")\n    if INDEX_BASLANGIC not in icerik or INDEX_BITIS not in icerik:\n        return\n\n    bas = icerik.index(INDEX_BASLANGIC) + len(INDEX_BASLANGIC)\n    son = icerik.index(INDEX_BITIS)\n    mevcut = [s for s in icerik[bas:son].strip().splitlines() if s.strip().startswith(\"-\")]\n\n    yeni_satir = f\"- {tarih} — [{dosya_adi}](oturumlar/{dosya_adi}) — {ozet}\"\n    mevcut = [s for s in mevcut if f\"(oturumlar/{dosya_adi})\" not in s]\n    mevcut.append(yeni_satir)\n    mevcut.sort(reverse=True)\n\n    yeni = icerik[:bas] + \"\\n\\n\" + \"\\n\".join(mevcut) + \"\\n\\n\" + icerik[son:]\n    gunluk.write_text(yeni, encoding=\"utf-8\")\n\n\ndef ozet_uret(turler) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Indekste gorunecek kisa etiket: ilk kullanici mesajinin bas kismi.\"\"\"\n    for rol, _, metin in turler:\n        if rol == \"user\":\n            tek_satir = re.sub(r\"\\s+\", \" \", metin).strip()\n            return tek_satir[:80] + (\"…\" if len(tek_satir) > 80 else \"\")\n    return \"(bos oturum)\"\n\n\ndef git(kok: Path, *argumanlar) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess:\n    return subprocess.run(\n        [\"git\", *argumanlar], cwd=kok, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=120\n    )\n\n\ndef gonder(kok: Path, oturum_id: str) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Sadece kayit dosyalarini commit'leyip uzak dala gonderir.\"\"\"\n    dal = git(kok, \"rev-parse\", \"--abbrev-ref\", \"HEAD\").stdout.strip()\n    if not dal or dal == \"HEAD\":\n        return\n\n    git(kok, \"add\", \"--\", \"docs/oturumlar\", \"docs/konusma-gunlugu.md\")\n    degisiklik = git(kok, \"diff\", \"--cached\", \"--quiet\", \"--\", \"docs/oturumlar\", \"docs/konusma-gunlugu.md\")\n    if degisiklik.returncode == 0:\n        return  # stage'de fark yok, yapilacak bir sey de yok\n\n    git(kok, \"commit\", \"-m\", f\"chore(kayit): oturum kaydi guncellendi ({oturum_id[:8]})\")\n\n    for bekleme in (0, 2, 4, 8, 16):\n        if bekleme:\n            import time\n\n            time.sleep(bekleme)\n        if git(kok, \"push\", \"-u\", \"origin\", dal).returncode == 0:\n            return\n\n\ndef main() -> int:\n    try:\n        girdi = json.load(sys.stdin)\n    except (json.JSONDecodeError, ValueError):\n        girdi = {}\n\n    transcript = girdi.get(\"transcript_path\")\n    oturum_id = girdi.get(\"session_id\") or \"bilinmeyen\"\n    if not transcript or not Path(transcript).exists():\n        return 0\n\n    turler = turlari_oku(Path(transcript))\n    if not turler:\n        return 0\n\n    kok = repo_koku()\n    tarih = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime(\"%Y-%m-%d\")\n    dosya_adi = f\"{tarih}-{oturum_id[:8]}.md\"\n\n    oturumlar = kok / \"docs\" / \"oturumlar\"\n    ham = oturumlar / \"ham\"\n    oturumlar.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    ham.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n\n    # Iki dosya da repoya push edildigi icin yazmadan once maskelenir.\n    (oturumlar / dosya_adi).write_text(\n        gizlileri_maskele(markdown_uret(turler, oturum_id, tarih)), encoding=\"utf-8\"\n    )\n    (ham / f\"{tarih}-{oturum_id[:8]}.jsonl\").write_text(\n        gizlileri_maskele(Path(transcript).read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\", errors=\"replace\")),\n        encoding=\"utf-8\",\n    )\n    indeksi_guncelle(\n        kok / \"docs\" / \"konusma-gunlugu.md\",\n        dosya_adi,\n        tarih,\n        gizlileri_maskele(ozet_uret(turler)),\n    )\n\n    if \"--push\" in sys.argv:\n        gonder(kok, oturum_id)\n\n    return 0\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    sys.exit(main())\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":152,"oldLines":7,"newStart":152,"newLines":9,"lines":["     \"\"\"Indekste gorunecek kisa etiket: ilk kullanici mesajinin bas -{"parentUuid":"90b20c0b-735a-47b7-a043-c758aa2a9083","isSidechain":false,"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_<ID>","type":"tool_result","content":"The file /home/user/cloud/.claude/hooks/oturum-kaydet.py has been updated successfully. (file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)"}]},"uuid":"efcb9160-979b-4068-bab5-0fb5a62f3f0b","timestamp":"2026-08-09T21:52:22.798Z","toolUseResult":{"filePath":"/home/user/cloud/.claude/hooks/oturum-kaydet.py","oldString":"    indeksi_guncelle(\n        kok / \"docs\" / \"konusma-gunlugu.md\",\n        dosya_adi,\n        tarih,\n        gizlileri_maskele(ozet_uret(turler)),\n    )","newString":"    indeksi_guncelle(kok / \"docs\" / \"konusma-gunlugu.md\", dosya_adi, tarih, ozet_uret(turler))","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Oturum kaydedici hook.\n\nClaude Code'un Stop / SessionEnd olaylarinda calisir. stdin'den gelen hook\nJSON'undaki transcript dosyasini okur ve iki sey uretir:\n\n  docs/oturumlar/<tarih>-<oturum>.md    okunabilir konusma dokumu\n  docs/oturumlar/ham/<tarih>-<oturum>.jsonl   ham transcript yedegi\n\nAyrica docs/konusma-gunlugu.md dosyasindaki oturum indeksini gunceller.\n\n--push verildiginde (SessionEnd) yazilan dosyalari commit'leyip uzak dala\ngonderir. Sadece docs/oturumlar ve docs/konusma-gunlugu.md yollarini\nstage'ler, boylece devam eden calismadaki dosyalara dokunmaz.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport os\nimport re\nimport subprocess\nimport sys\nfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nINDEX_BASLANGIC = \"<!-- oturumlar:baslangic -->\"\nINDEX_BITIS = \"<!-- oturumlar:bitis -->\"\n\n\ndef repo_koku() -> Path:\n    \"\"\"Depo kokunu bul: once CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR, sonra script konumu.\"\"\"\n    ortam = os.environ.get(\"CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR\")\n    if ortam and (Path(ortam) / \".git\").exists():\n        return Path(ortam)\n    return Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]\n\n\n# Konusma dokumu repoya push edildigi icin, konusmada gecen anahtar\n# gorunumlu diziler yazilmadan once maskelenir. Aksi halde GitHub'in push\n# korumasi butun push'u reddediyor (ve hakli olarak).\nGIZLI_DESENLER = re.compile(\n    r\"\"\"(\n        sk-ant-[A-Za-z0-9_-]{16,}          # Anthropic\n      | sk_live_[A-Za-z0-9]{16,}           # Stripe canli\n      | sk_test_[A-Za-z0-9]{16,}           # Stripe test\n      | rk_live_[A-Za-z0-9]{16,}           # Stripe kisitli\n      | AKIA[0-9A-Z]{16}                   # AWS erisim anahtari\n      | ASIA[0-9A-Z]{16}                   # AWS gecici anahtar\n      | gh[pousr]_[A-Za-z0-9]{20,}         # GitHub token\n      | github_pat_[A-Za-z0-9_]{20,}       # GitHub ince taneli PAT\n      | glpat-[A-Za-z0-9_-]{16,}           # GitLab\n      | xox[abprs]-[A-Za-z0-9-]{10,}       # Slack\n      | AIza[0-9A-Za-z_-]{35}              # Google API\n      | -----BEGIN[ A-Z]*PRIVATE\\ KEY----- # Ozel anahtar blogu\n    )\"\"\",\n    re.VERBOSE,\n)\n\n\ndef gizlileri_maskele(metin: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Anahtar gorunumlu dizileri maskeler; kalanini oldugu gibi birakir.\"\"\"\n    return GIZLI_DESENLER.sub(\"<REDACTED>\", metin)\n\n\ndef metin_bloklari(icerik) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Bir mesajin icerigindeki duz metin bloklarini birlestirir.\n\n    Arac cagrilari (tool_use / tool_result) ve dusunme bloklari atlanir --\n    amac konusmanin kendisini saklamak, her ara adimi degil.\n    \"\"\"\n    if isinstance(icerik, str):\n        return icerik.strip()\n    if not isinstance(icerik, list):\n        return \"\"\n    parcalar = []\n    for blok in icerik:\n        if isinstance(blok, dict) and blok.get(\"type\") == \"text\":\n            parcalar.append(str(blok.get(\"text\", \"\")).strip())\n    return \"\\n\\n\".join(p for p in parcalar if p)\n\n\ndef turlari_oku(transcript: Path):\n    \"\"\"Transcript jsonl dosyasini (rol, zaman, metin) uclulerine cevirir.\"\"\"\n    turler = []\n    with transcript.open(encoding=\"utf-8\", errors=\"replace\") as f:\n        for satir in f:\n            satir = satir.strip()\n            if not satir:\n                continue\n            try:\n                kayit = json.loads(satir)\n            except json.JSONDecodeError:\n                continue\n            if kayit.get(\"isSidechain\") or kayit.get(\"isMeta\"):\n                continue\n            tur = kayit.get(\"type\")\n            if tur not in (\"user\", \"assistant\"):\n                continue\n            mesaj = kayit.get(\"message\") or {}\n            metin = metin_bloklari(mesaj.get(\"content\"))\n            if not metin:\n                continue\n            # Arac sonuclarini tasiyan sistem hatirlatmalarini disarida birak.\n            if metin.startswith(\"<system-reminder>\") or metin.startswith(\"<command-name>\"):\n                continue\n            turler.append((tur, kayit.get(\"timestamp\", \"\"), metin))\n    return turler\n\n\ndef markdown_uret(turler, oturum_id: str, tarih: str) -> str:\n    satirlar = [\n        f\"# Oturum {tarih}\",\n        \"\",\n        f\"- Oturum kimligi: `{oturum_id}`\",\n        f\"- Son guncelleme: {datetime.now(timezone.utc):%Y-%m-%d %H:%M} UTC\",\n        f\"- Tur sayisi: {len(turler)}\",\n        \"\",\n        \"---\",\n        \"\",\n    ]\n    for rol, zaman, metin in turler:\n        baslik = \"Ben\" if rol == \"user\" else \"Claude\"\n        damga = f\" _{zaman[:19].replace('T', ' ')} UTC_\" if zaman else \"\"\n        satirlar.append(f\"## {baslik}{damga}\")\n        satirlar.append(\"\")\n        satirlar.append(metin)\n        satirlar.append(\"\")\n    return \"\\n\".join(satirlar).rstrip() + \"\\n\"\n\n\ndef indeksi_guncelle(gunluk: Path, dosya_adi: str, tarih: str, ozet: str) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Konusma gunlugundeki isaretli blogu tarihe gore siralayarak gunceller.\"\"\"\n    if not gunluk.exists():\n        return\n    icerik = gunluk.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\")\n    if INDEX_BASLANGIC not in icerik or INDEX_BITIS not in icerik:\n        return\n\n    bas = icerik.index(INDEX_BASLANGIC) + len(INDEX_BASLANGIC)\n    son = icerik.index(INDEX_BITIS)\n    mevcut = [s for s in icerik[bas:son].strip().splitlines() if s.strip().startswith(\"-\")]\n\n    yeni_satir = f\"- {tarih} — [{dosya_adi}](oturumlar/{dosya_adi}) — {ozet}\"\n    mevcut = [s for s in mevcut if f\"(oturumlar/{dosya_adi})\" not in s]\n    mevcut.append(yeni_satir)\n    mevcut.sort(reverse=True)\n\n    yeni = icerik[:bas] + \"\\n\\n\" + \"\\n\".join(mevcut) + \"\\n\\n\" + icerik[son:]\n    gunluk.write_text(yeni, encoding=\"utf-8\")\n\n\ndef ozet_uret(turler) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Indekste gorunecek kisa etiket: ilk kullanici mesajinin bas kismi.\"\"\"\n    for rol, _, metin in turler:\n        if rol == \"user\":\n            # Kirpmadan once maskele: 80 karakterde kesilen bir anahtar artik\n            # desene uymaz ve yarisi indekste kalirdi.\n            tek_satir = re.sub(r\"\\s+\", \" \", gizlileri_maskele(metin)).strip()\n            return tek_satir[:80] + (\"…\" if len(tek_satir) > 80 else \"\")\n    return \"(bos oturum)\"\n\n\ndef git(kok: Path, *argumanlar) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess:\n    return subprocess.run(\n        [\"git\", *argumanlar], cwd=kok, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=120\n    )\n\n\ndef gonder(kok: Path, oturum_id: str) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Sadece kayit dosyalarini commit'leyip uzak dala gonderir.\"\"\"\n    dal = git(kok, \"rev-parse\", \"--abbrev-ref\", \"HEAD\").stdout.strip()\n    if not dal or dal == \"HEAD\":\n        return\n\n    git(kok, \"add\", \"--\", \"docs/oturumlar\", \"docs/konusma-gunlugu.md\")\n    degisiklik = git(kok, \"diff\", \"--cached\", \"--quiet\", \"--\", \"docs/oturumlar\", \"docs/konusma-gunlugu.md\")\n    if degisiklik.returncode == 0:\n        return  # stage'de fark yok, yapilacak bir sey de yok\n\n    git(kok, \"commit\", \"-m\", f\"chore(kayit): oturum kaydi guncellendi ({oturum_id[:8]})\")\n\n    for bekleme in (0, 2, 4, 8, 16):\n        if bekleme:\n            import time\n\n            time.sleep(bekleme)\n        if git(kok, \"push\", \"-u\", \"origin\", dal).returncode == 0:\n            return\n\n\ndef main() -> int:\n    try:\n        girdi = json.load(sys.stdin)\n    except (json.JSONDecodeError, ValueError):\n        girdi = {}\n\n    transcript = girdi.get(\"transcript_path\")\n    oturum_id = girdi.get(\"session_id\") or \"bilinmeyen\"\n    if not transcript or not Path(transcript).exists():\n        return 0\n\n    turler = turlari_oku(Path(transcript))\n    if not turler:\n        return 0\n\n    kok = repo_koku()\n    tarih = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime(\"%Y-%m-%d\")\n    dosya_adi = f\"{tarih}-{oturum_id[:8]}.md\"\n\n    oturumlar = kok / \"docs\" / \"oturumlar\"\n    ham = oturumlar / \"ham\"\n    oturumlar.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    ham.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n\n    # Iki dosya da repoya push edildigi icin yazmadan once maskelenir.\n    (oturumlar / dosya_adi).write_text(\n        gizlileri_maskele(markdown_uret(turler, oturum_id, tarih)), encoding=\"utf-8\"\n    )\n    (ham / f\"{tarih}-{oturum_id[:8]}.jsonl\").write_text(\n        gizlileri_maskele(Path(transcript).read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\", errors=\"replace\")),\n        encoding=\"utf-8\",\n    )\n    indeksi_guncelle(\n        kok / \"docs\" / \"konusma-gunlugu.md\",\n        dosya_adi,\n        tarih,\n        gizlileri_maskele(ozet_uret(turler)),\n    )\n\n    if \"--push\" in sys.argv:\n        gonder(kok, oturum_id)\n\n    return 0\n\n\nif __name__ == +{"parentUuid":"90b20c0b-735a-47b7-a043-c758aa2a9083","isSidechain":false,"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_<ID>","type":"tool_result","content":"The file /home/user/cloud/.claude/hooks/oturum-kaydet.py has been updated successfully. (file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)"}]},"uuid":"efcb9160-979b-4068-bab5-0fb5a62f3f0b","timestamp":"2026-08-09T21:52:22.798Z","toolUseResult":{"filePath":"/home/user/cloud/.claude/hooks/oturum-kaydet.py","oldString":"    indeksi_guncelle(\n        kok / \"docs\" / \"konusma-gunlugu.md\",\n        dosya_adi,\n        tarih,\n        gizlileri_maskele(ozet_uret(turler)),\n    )","newString":"    indeksi_guncelle(kok / \"docs\" / \"konusma-gunlugu.md\", dosya_adi, tarih, ozet_uret(turler))","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Oturum kaydedici hook.\n\nClaude Code'un Stop / SessionEnd olaylarinda calisir. stdin'den gelen hook\nJSON'undaki transcript dosyasini okur ve iki sey uretir:\n\n  docs/oturumlar/<tarih>-<oturum>.md    okunabilir konusma dokumu\n  docs/oturumlar/ham/<tarih>-<oturum>.jsonl   ham transcript yedegi\n\nAyrica docs/konusma-gunlugu.md dosyasindaki oturum indeksini gunceller.\n\n--push verildiginde (SessionEnd) yazilan dosyalari commit'leyip uzak dala\ngonderir. Sadece docs/oturumlar ve docs/konusma-gunlugu.md yollarini\nstage'ler, boylece devam eden calismadaki dosyalara dokunmaz.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport os\nimport re\nimport subprocess\nimport sys\nfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nINDEX_BASLANGIC = \"<!-- oturumlar:baslangic -->\"\nINDEX_BITIS = \"<!-- oturumlar:bitis -->\"\n\n\ndef repo_koku() -> Path:\n    \"\"\"Depo kokunu bul: once CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR, sonra script konumu.\"\"\"\n    ortam = os.environ.get(\"CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR\")\n    if ortam and (Path(ortam) / \".git\").exists():\n        return Path(ortam)\n    return Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]\n\n\n# Konusma dokumu repoya push edildigi icin, konusmada gecen anahtar\n# gorunumlu diziler yazilmadan once maskelenir. Aksi halde GitHub'in push\n# korumasi butun push'u reddediyor (ve hakli olarak).\nGIZLI_DESENLER = re.compile(\n    r\"\"\"(\n        sk-ant-[A-Za-z0-9_-]{16,}          # Anthropic\n      | sk_live_[A-Za-z0-9]{16,}           # Stripe canli\n      | sk_test_[A-Za-z0-9]{16,}           # Stripe test\n      | rk_live_[A-Za-z0-9]{16,}           # Stripe kisitli\n      | AKIA[0-9A-Z]{16}                   # AWS erisim anahtari\n      | ASIA[0-9A-Z]{16}                   # AWS gecici anahtar\n      | gh[pousr]_[A-Za-z0-9]{20,}         # GitHub token\n      | github_pat_[A-Za-z0-9_]{20,}       # GitHub ince taneli PAT\n      | glpat-[A-Za-z0-9_-]{16,}           # GitLab\n      | xox[abprs]-[A-Za-z0-9-]{10,}       # Slack\n      | AIza[0-9A-Za-z_-]{35}              # Google API\n      | -----BEGIN[ A-Z]*PRIVATE\\ KEY----- # Ozel anahtar blogu\n    )\"\"\",\n    re.VERBOSE,\n)\n\n\ndef gizlileri_maskele(metin: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Anahtar gorunumlu dizileri maskeler; kalanini oldugu gibi birakir.\"\"\"\n    return GIZLI_DESENLER.sub(\"<REDACTED>\", metin)\n\n\ndef metin_bloklari(icerik) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Bir mesajin icerigindeki duz metin bloklarini birlestirir.\n\n    Arac cagrilari (tool_use / tool_result) ve dusunme bloklari atlanir --\n    amac konusmanin kendisini saklamak, her ara adimi degil.\n    \"\"\"\n    if isinstance(icerik, str):\n        return icerik.strip()\n    if not isinstance(icerik, list):\n        return \"\"\n    parcalar = []\n    for blok in icerik:\n        if isinstance(blok, dict) and blok.get(\"type\") == \"text\":\n            parcalar.append(str(blok.get(\"text\", \"\")).strip())\n    return \"\\n\\n\".join(p for p in parcalar if p)\n\n\ndef turlari_oku(transcript: Path):\n    \"\"\"Transcript jsonl dosyasini (rol, zaman, metin) uclulerine cevirir.\"\"\"\n    turler = []\n    with transcript.open(encoding=\"utf-8\", errors=\"replace\") as f:\n        for satir in f:\n            satir = satir.strip()\n            if not satir:\n                continue\n            try:\n                kayit = json.loads(satir)\n            except json.JSONDecodeError:\n                continue\n            if kayit.get(\"isSidechain\") or kayit.get(\"isMeta\"):\n                continue\n            tur = kayit.get(\"type\")\n            if tur not in (\"user\", \"assistant\"):\n                continue\n            mesaj = kayit.get(\"message\") or {}\n            metin = metin_bloklari(mesaj.get(\"content\"))\n            if not metin:\n                continue\n            # Arac sonuclarini tasiyan sistem hatirlatmalarini disarida birak.\n            if metin.startswith(\"<system-reminder>\") or metin.startswith(\"<command-name>\"):\n                continue\n            turler.append((tur, kayit.get(\"timestamp\", \"\"), metin))\n    return turler\n\n\ndef markdown_uret(turler, oturum_id: str, tarih: str) -> str:\n    satirlar = [\n        f\"# Oturum {tarih}\",\n        \"\",\n        f\"- Oturum kimligi: `{oturum_id}`\",\n        f\"- Son guncelleme: {datetime.now(timezone.utc):%Y-%m-%d %H:%M} UTC\",\n        f\"- Tur sayisi: {len(turler)}\",\n        \"\",\n        \"---\",\n        \"\",\n    ]\n    for rol, zaman, metin in turler:\n        baslik = \"Ben\" if rol == \"user\" else \"Claude\"\n        damga = f\" _{zaman[:19].replace('T', ' ')} UTC_\" if zaman else \"\"\n        satirlar.append(f\"## {baslik}{damga}\")\n        satirlar.append(\"\")\n        satirlar.append(metin)\n        satirlar.append(\"\")\n    return \"\\n\".join(satirlar).rstrip() + \"\\n\"\n\n\ndef indeksi_guncelle(gunluk: Path, dosya_adi: str, tarih: str, ozet: str) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Konusma gunlugundeki isaretli blogu tarihe gore siralayarak gunceller.\"\"\"\n    if not gunluk.exists():\n        return\n    icerik = gunluk.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\")\n    if INDEX_BASLANGIC not in icerik or INDEX_BITIS not in icerik:\n        return\n\n    bas = icerik.index(INDEX_BASLANGIC) + len(INDEX_BASLANGIC)\n    son = icerik.index(INDEX_BITIS)\n    mevcut = [s for s in icerik[bas:son].strip().splitlines() if s.strip().startswith(\"-\")]\n\n    yeni_satir = f\"- {tarih} — [{dosya_adi}](oturumlar/{dosya_adi}) — {ozet}\"\n    mevcut = [s for s in mevcut if f\"(oturumlar/{dosya_adi})\" not in s]\n    mevcut.append(yeni_satir)\n    mevcut.sort(reverse=True)\n\n    yeni = icerik[:bas] + \"\\n\\n\" + \"\\n\".join(mevcut) + \"\\n\\n\" + icerik[son:]\n    gunluk.write_text(yeni, encoding=\"utf-8\")\n\n\ndef ozet_uret(turler) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Indekste gorunecek kisa etiket: ilk kullanici mesajinin bas kismi.\"\"\"\n    for rol, _, metin in turler:\n        if rol == \"user\":\n            # Kirpmadan once maskele: 80 karakterde kesilen bir anahtar artik\n            # desene uymaz ve yarisi indekste kalirdi.\n            tek_satir = re.sub(r\"\\s+\", \" \", gizlileri_maskele(metin)).strip()\n            return tek_satir[:80] + (\"…\" if len(tek_satir) > 80 else \"\")\n    return \"(bos oturum)\"\n\n\ndef git(kok: Path, *argumanlar) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess:\n    return subprocess.run(\n        [\"git\", *argumanlar], cwd=kok, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=120\n    )\n\n\ndef gonder(kok: Path, oturum_id: str) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Sadece kayit dosyalarini commit'leyip uzak dala gonderir.\"\"\"\n    dal = git(kok, \"rev-parse\", \"--abbrev-ref\", \"HEAD\").stdout.strip()\n    if not dal or dal == \"HEAD\":\n        return\n\n    git(kok, \"add\", \"--\", \"docs/oturumlar\", \"docs/konusma-gunlugu.md\")\n    degisiklik = git(kok, \"diff\", \"--cached\", \"--quiet\", \"--\", \"docs/oturumlar\", \"docs/konusma-gunlugu.md\")\n    if degisiklik.returncode == 0:\n        return  # stage'de fark yok, yapilacak bir sey de yok\n\n    git(kok, \"commit\", \"-m\", f\"chore(kayit): oturum kaydi guncellendi ({oturum_id[:8]})\")\n\n    for bekleme in (0, 2, 4, 8, 16):\n        if bekleme:\n            import time\n\n            time.sleep(bekleme)\n        if git(kok, \"push\", \"-u\", \"origin\", dal).returncode == 0:\n            return\n\n\ndef main() -> int:\n    try:\n        girdi = json.load(sys.stdin)\n    except (json.JSONDecodeError, ValueError):\n        girdi = {}\n\n    transcript = girdi.get(\"transcript_path\")\n    oturum_id = girdi.get(\"session_id\") or \"bilinmeyen\"\n    if not transcript or not Path(transcript).exists():\n        return 0\n\n    turler = turlari_oku(Path(transcript))\n    if not turler:\n        return 0\n\n    kok = repo_koku()\n    tarih = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime(\"%Y-%m-%d\")\n    dosya_adi = f\"{tarih}-{oturum_id[:8]}.md\"\n\n    oturumlar = kok / \"docs\" / \"oturumlar\"\n    ham = oturumlar / \"ham\"\n    oturumlar.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    ham.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n\n    # Iki dosya da repoya push edildigi icin yazmadan once maskelenir.\n    (oturumlar / dosya_adi).write_text(\n        gizlileri_maskele(markdown_uret(turler, oturum_id, tarih)), encoding=\"utf-8\"\n    )\n    (ham / f\"{tarih}-{oturum_id[:8]}.jsonl\").write_text(\n        gizlileri_maskele(Path(transcript).read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\", errors=\"replace\")),\n        encoding=\"utf-8\",\n    )\n    indeksi_guncelle(\n        kok / \"docs\" / \"konusma-gunlugu.md\",\n        dosya_adi,\n        tarih,\n        gizlileri_maskele(ozet_uret(turler)),\n    )\n\n    if \"--push\" in sys.argv:\n        gonder(kok, oturum_id)\n\n    return 0\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    sys.exit(main())\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":219,"oldLines":12,"newStart":219,"newLines":7,"lines":["         gizlileri_maskele(Path(transcript).read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\", errors=\"replace\")),","         encoding=\"utf-8\",","     )","-    indeksi_guncelle(","-        kok / \"docs\" / \"konusma-gunlugu.md\",","-        dosya_adi,","-        tarih,","-  {"parentUuid":"074a772a-70cc-4ca5-b62d-9d738a9e08b5","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"agent_listing_delta","addedTypes":["claude","claude-code-guide","Explore","general-purpose","Plan","statusline-setup"],"addedLines":["- claude: Catch-all for any task that doesn't fit a more specific agent. FleetView's default when no agent name is typed. (Tools: *)","- claude-code-guide: Use this agent when the user asks questions (\"Can Claude...\", \"Does Claude...\", \"How do I...\") about: (1) Claude Code (the CLI tool) - features, hooks, slash commands, MCP servers, settings, IDE integrations, keyboard shortcuts; (2) Claude Agent SDK - building custom agents; (3) Claude API (formerly Anthropic API) - Messages API for directly passing messages to Claude, Tool Runner (`client.beta.messages.tool_runner`) for running an agentic loop over your own tools, manual tool-use loops, Managed Agents for server-hosted agents with a managed sandbox, prompt caching, and general Anthropic SDK usage; (4) Claude Tag (Claude in Slack) - what it is, setting it up for a Slack workspace, `/install-slack-app`. **IMPORTANT:** Before spawning a new agent, check if there is already a running or recently completed claude-code-guide agent that you can continue via SendMessage. (Tools: Glob, Grep, Read, WebFetch, WebSearch)","- Explore: Read-only search agent for broad fan-out searches — when answering means sweeping many files, directories, or naming conventions and you only need the conclusion, not the file dumps. It reads excerpts rather than whole files, so it locates code; it doesn't review or audit it. Specify search breadth: \"medium\" for moderate exploration, \"very thorough\" for multiple locations and naming conventions. (Tools: All tools except Agent, Artifact, ExitPlanMode, Edit, Write, NotebookEdit)","- general-purpose: General-purpose agent for researching complex questions, searching for code, and executing multi-step tasks. When you are searching for a keyword or file and are not confident that you will find the right match in the first few tries use this agent to perform the search for you. (Tools: *)","- Plan: Software architect agent for designing implementation plans. Use this when you need to plan the implementation strategy for a task. Returns step-by-step plans, identifies critical files, and considers architectural trade-offs. (Tools: All tools except Agent, Artifact, ExitPlanMode, Edit, Write, NotebookEdit)","- statusline-setup: Use this agent to configure the user's Claude Code status line setting. (Tools: Read, Edit)"],"removedTypes":[],"isInitial":true,"showConcurrencyNote":false},"type":"attachment","uuid":"65c7fbba-b58b-4682-b87a-c8beb65fc3a6","timestamp":"2026-08-09T21:52:22.818Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"remote_desktop","cwd":"/home/user/cloud","sessionId":"bb3bdf55-9295-5e36-8253-c157b260d2a3","version":"2.1.226","gitBranch":"claude/code-review-plugin-jvgabq"}

 {"parentUuid":"39f9e491-c801-4b90-ae48-e529e5e20d0e","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"agent_listing_delta","addedTypes":["claude","Explore","general-purpose","Plan","statusline-setup"],"addedLines":["- claude: Catch-all for any task that doesn't fit a more specific agent. FleetView's default when no agent name is typed. (Tools: *)","- Explore: Fast read-only search agent for locating code. Use it to find files by pattern (eg. \"src/components/**/*.tsx\"), grep for symbols or keywords (eg. \"API endpoints\"), or answer \"where is X defined / which files reference Y.\" Do NOT use it for code review, design-doc auditing, cross-file consistency checks, or open-ended analysis — it reads excerpts rather than whole files and will miss content past its read window. When calling, specify search breadth: \"quick\" for a single targeted lookup, \"medium\" for moderate exploration, or \"very thorough\" to search across multiple locations and naming conventions. (Tools: All tools except Agent, Artifact, ExitPlanMode, Edit, Write, NotebookEdit)","- general-purpose: General-purpose agent for researching complex questions, searching for code, and executing multi-step tasks. When you are searching for a keyword or file and are not confident that you will find the right match in the first few tries use this agent to perform the search for you. (Tools: *)","- Plan: Software architect agent for designing implementation plans. Use this when you need to plan the implementation strategy for a task. Returns step-by-step plans, identifies critical files, and considers architectural trade-offs. (Tools: All tools except Agent, Artifact, ExitPlanMode, Edit, Write, NotebookEdit)","- statusline-setup: Use this agent to configure the user's Claude Code status line setting.  {"parentUuid":"2dfd59a6-95e1-4104-bed7-073baf5cb452","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"skill_listing","content":"- dataviz: Use this skill whenever you are about to create ANY chart, graph, plot, dashboard, or data visualization, in ANY output medium — an HTML or React artifact, inline SVG, plotting code in any library (matplotlib, plotly, d3, Recharts, …), an image/PNG you will render and upload, or a chart shared into Slack. Read it BEFORE writing the first line of chart code, choosing chart colors, building a stat tile / meter / KPI row, or laying out a dashboard. Produces visualizations that read as one system — elegant, accessible, consistent in light and dark — using a brand-neutral placeholder palette you swap for your own. Teaches a design-system-agnostic method: a form heuristic, a color formula with a runnable validator, mark specs, and interaction rules. A validated default palette is documented in `references/palette.md` — swap that file's values for your brand's. Triggers on: \"chart\", \"graph\", \"plot\", \"data viz\", \"visualization\", \"dashboard\", \"analytics\", \"visualize data\", \"categorical colors\", \"sequential / diverging palette\", \"stat tile\", \"sparkline\", \"heatmap\", \"legend\", \"axis\", \"tooltip\", \"chart colors\", \"color by series\".\n- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- code-review: Review the current diff, or a PR number/branch/path target, for correctness bugs and reuse/simplification/efficiency cleanups at the given effort level (low/medium: fewer, high-confidence findings; high→max: broader coverage, may include uncertain findings); with no level given, it reuses the level you typed last. Pass --comment to post findings as inline PR comments, or --fix to apply the findings to the working tree after the review.\n- simplify: Review the changed code for reuse, simplification, efficiency, and altitude cleanups, then apply the fixes. Quality only — it does not hunt for bugs; use /code-review for that.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- claude-api: Reference for the Claude API / Anthropic SDK — model ids, pricing, params, streaming, tool use, MCP, agents, caching, token counting, model migration.\nTRIGGER — read BEFORE opening the target file; don't skip because it \"looks like a one-liner\" — whenever: the prompt names Claude/Anthropic in any form (Claude, Anthropic, Fable, Opus, Sonnet, Haiku, `anthropic`, `@anthropic-ai`, `claude-*`, `us.anthropic.*`, `[1m]`); the user asks about an LLM (pricing/model choice/limits/caching) — never answer from memory; OR the task is LLM-shaped with provider unstated (agent/MCP/tool-definition/multi-agent/RAG/LLM-judge/computer-use; generate/summarize/extract/classify/rewrite/converse over NL; debugging refusals/cutoffs/streaming/tool-calls/tokens).\nSKIP only when another provider is being worked on (overrides all triggers): OpenAI/GPT/Gemini/Llama/Mistral/Cohere/Ollama named in the query; OR `grep -rE 'openai|langchain_openai|google.generativeai|genai|mistralai|cohere|ollama'` over the project hits (run this grep FIRST if no provider named — don't Read the file).\n- run: Launch and drive this project's app to see a change working. Use when asked to run, start, or screenshot the app, or to confirm a change works in the real app (not just tests). First looks for a project skill that already covers launching the app; otherwise falls back to built-in patterns per project type (CLI, server, TUI, Electron, browser-driven, library).\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch","skillCount":11,"isInitial":true,"names":["dataviz","update-config","keybindings-help","code-review","simplify","fewer-permission-prompts","loop","claude-api","run","init","security-review"]},"type":"attachment","uuid":"5b72dd2f-6bf9-43c7-a92d-6e87fe18daf4","timestamp":"2026-08-09T21:54:15.666Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"sdk-py","cwd":"/home/user/cloud","sessionId":"bb3bdf55-9295-5e36-8253-c157b260d2a3","version":"2.1.226","gitBranch":"claude/code-review-plugin-jvgabq"}

Sadece docs/oturumlar ve docs/konusma-gunlugu.md yollarini\n14\tstage'ler, boylece devam eden calismadaki dosyalara dokunmaz.\n15\t\"\"\"\n16\t\n17\timport json\n18\timport os\n19\timport re\n20\timport subprocess\n21\timport sys\n22\tfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\n23\tfrom pathlib import Path\n24\t\n25\tINDEX_BASLANGIC = \"<!-- oturumlar:baslangic -->\"\n26\tINDEX_BITIS = \"<!-- oturumlar:bitis -->\"\n27\t\n28\t\n29\tdef repo_koku() -> Path:\n30\t    \"\"\"Depo kokunu bul: once CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR, sonra script konumu.\"\"\"\n31\t    ortam = os.environ.get(\"CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR\")\n32\t    if ortam and (Path(ortam) / \".git\").exists():\n33\t        return Path(ortam)\n34\t    return Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]\n35\t\n36\t\n37\t# Konusma dokumu repoya push edildigi icin, konusmada gecen anahtar\n38\t# gorunumlu diziler yazilmadan once maskelenir. Aksi halde GitHub'in push\n39\t# korumasi butun push'u reddediyor (ve hakli olarak).\n40\tGIZLI_DESENLER = re.compile(\n41\t    r\"\"\"(\n42\t        sk-ant-[A-Za-z0-9_-]{16,}          # Anthropic\n43\t      | sk_live_[A-Za-z0-9]{16,}           # Stripe canli\n44\t      | sk_test_[A-Za-z0-9]{16,}           # Stripe test\n45\t      | rk_live_[A-Za-z0-9]{16,}           # Stripe kisitli\n46\t      | AKIA[0-9A-Z]{16}                   # AWS erisim anahtari\n47\t      | ASIA[0-9A-Z]{16}                   # AWS gecici anahtar\n48\t      | gh[pousr]_[A-Za-z0-9]{20,}         # GitHub token\n49\t      | github_pat_[A-Za-z0-9_]{20,}       # GitHub ince taneli PAT\n50\t      | glpat-[A-Za-z0-9_-]{16,}           # GitLab\n51\t      | xox[abprs]-[A-Za-z0-9-]{10,}       # Slack\n52\t      | AIza[0-9A-Za-z_-]{35}              # Google API\n53\t      | -----BEGIN[ A-Z]*PRIVATE\\ KEY----- # Ozel anahtar blogu\n54\t    )\"\"\",\n55\t    re.VERBOSE,\n56\t)\n57\t\n58\t\n59\tdef gizlileri_maskele(metin: str) -> str:\n60\t    \"\"\"Anahtar gorunumlu dizileri maskeler; kalanini oldugu gibi birakir.\"\"\"\n61\t    return GIZLI_DESENLER.sub(\"<REDACTED>\", metin)\n62\t\n63\t\n64\tdef metin_bloklari(icerik) -> str:\n65\t    \"\"\"Bir mesajin icerigindeki duz metin bloklarini birlestirir.\n66\t\n67\t    Arac cagrilari (tool_use / tool_result) ve dusunme bloklari atlanir --\n68\t    amac konusmanin kendisini saklamak, her ara adimi degil.\n69\t    \"\"\"\n70\t    if isinstance(icerik, str):\n71\t        return icerik.strip()\n72\t    if not isinstance(icerik, list):\n73\t        return \"\"\n74\t    parcalar = []\n75\t    for blok in icerik:\n76\t        if isinstance(blok, dict) and blok.get(\"type\") == \"text\":\n77\t            parcalar.append(str(blok.get(\"text\", \"\")).strip())\n78\t    return \"\\n\\n\".join(p for p in parcalar if p)\n79\t\n80\t\n81\tdef turlari_oku(transcript: Path):\n82\t    \"\"\"Transcript jsonl dosyasini (rol, zaman, metin) uclulerine cevirir.\"\"\"\n83\t    turler = []\n84\t    with transcript.open(encoding=\"utf-8\", errors=\"replace\") as f:\n85\t        for satir in f:\n86\t            satir = satir.strip()\n87\t            if not satir:\n88\t                continue\n89\t            try:\n90\t                kayit = json.loads(satir)\n91\t            except json.JSONDecodeError:\n92\t                continue\n93\t            if kayit.get(\"isSidechain\") or kayit.get(\"isMeta\"):\n94\t                continue\n95\t            tur = kayit.get(\"type\")\n96\t            if tur not in (\"user\", \"assistant\"):\n97\t                continue\n98\t            mesaj = kayit.get(\"message\") or {}\n99\t            metin = metin_bloklari(mesaj.get(\"content\"))\n100\t            if not metin:\n101\t                continue\n102\t            # Arac sonuclarini tasiyan sistem hatirlatmalarini disarida birak.\n103\t            if metin.startswith(\"<system-reminder>\") or metin.startswith(\"<command-name>\"):\n104\t                continue\n105\t            turler.append((tur, kayit.get(\"timestamp\", \"\"), metin))\n106\t    return turler\n107\t\n108\t\n109\tdef markdown_uret(turler, oturum_id: str, tarih: str) -> str:\n110\t    satirlar = [\n111\t        f\"# Oturum {tarih}\",\n112\t        \"\",\n113\t        f\"- Oturum kimligi: `{oturum_id}`\",\n114\t        f\"- Son guncelleme: {datetime.now(timezone.utc):%Y-%m-%d %H:%M} UTC\",\n115\t        f\"- Tur sayisi: {len(turler)}\",\n116\t        \"\",\n117\t        \"---\",\n118\t        \"\",\n119\t    ]\n120\t    for rol, zaman, metin in turler:\n121\t        baslik = \"Ben\" if rol == \"user\" else \"Claude\"\n122\t        damga = f\" _{zaman[:19].replace('T', ' ')} UTC_\" if zaman else \"\"\n123\t        satirlar.append(f\"## {baslik}{damga}\")\n124\t        satirlar.append(\"\")\n125\t        satirlar.append(metin)\n126\t        satirlar.append(\"\")\n127\t    return \"\\n\".join(satirlar).rstrip() + \"\\n\"\n128\t\n129\t\n130\tdef indeksi_guncelle(gunluk: Path, dosya_adi: str, tarih: str, ozet: str) -> None:\n131\t    \"\"\"Konusma gunlugundeki isaretli blogu tarihe gore siralayarak gunceller.\"\"\"\n132\t    if not gunluk.exists():\n133\t        return\n134\t    icerik = gunluk.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\")\n135\t    if INDEX_BASLANGIC not in icerik or INDEX_BITIS not in icerik:\n136\t        return\n137\t\n138\t    bas = icerik.index(INDEX_BASLANGIC) + len(INDEX_BASLANGIC)\n139\t    son = icerik.index(INDEX_BITIS)\n140\t    mevcut = [s for s in icerik[bas:son].strip().splitlines() if s.strip().startswith(\"-\")]\n141\t\n142\t    yeni_satir = f\"- {tarih} — [{dosya_adi}](oturumlar/{dosya_adi}) — {ozet}\"\n143\t    mevcut = [s for s in mevcut if f\"(oturumlar/{dosya_adi})\" not in s]\n144\t    mevcut.append(yeni_satir)\n145\t    mevcut.sort(reverse=True)\n146\t\n147\t    yeni = icerik[:bas] + \"\\n\\n\" + \"\\n\".join(mevcut) + \"\\n\\n\" + icerik[son:]\n148\t    gunluk.write_text(yeni, encoding=\"utf-8\")\n149\t\n150\t\n151\tdef ozet_uret(turler) -> str:\n152\t    \"\"\"Indekste gorunecek kisa etiket: ilk kullanici mesajinin bas kismi.\"\"\"\n153\t    for rol, _, metin in turler:\n154\t        if rol == \"user\":\n155\t            # Kirpmadan once maskele: 80 karakterde kesilen bir anahtar artik\n156\t            # desene uymaz ve yarisi indekste kalirdi.\n157\t            tek_satir = re.sub(r\"\\s+\", \" \", gizlileri_maskele(metin)).strip()\n158\t            return tek_satir[:80] + (\"…\" if len(tek_satir) > 80 else \"\")\n159\t    return \"(bos oturum)\"\n160\t\n161\t\n162\tdef git(kok: Path, *argumanlar) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess:\n163\t    return subprocess.run(\n164\t        [\"git\", *argumanlar], cwd=kok, capture_output=True,
